We will publicly release the AniMatrix model weights and inference code.

</details>


### [36] [Unified Multimodal Visual Tracking with Dual Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03716)
*Lingyi Hong,Jinglun Li,Xinyu Zhou,Kaixun Jiang,Pinxue Guo,Zhaoyu Chen,Runze Li,Xingdong Sheng,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OneTrackerV2是一个统一的多模态视觉目标追踪框架，通过Meta Merger实现多模态信息统一嵌入，并引入双专家混合架构（T-MoE处理时空关系，M-MoE处理多模态知识），在单一架构、统一参数和端到端训练下，在5种RGB和RGB+X追踪任务、12个基准上达到SOTA性能，同时保持高推理效率，且在模型压缩和模态缺失场景下表现鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对每种模态单独训练模型或依赖预训练模型适配新模态，导致效率低、可扩展性差、实用性受限，亟需一个统一的端到端多模态追踪框架。

Method: 提出OneTrackerV2框架，包含：(1) Meta Merger模块将多模态信息嵌入统一空间，实现灵活融合；(2) 双专家混合架构（DMoE），其中T-MoE建模时空关系用于追踪，M-MoE嵌入多模态知识以解耦跨模态依赖、减少特征冲突。

Result: 在5种RGB和RGB+X追踪任务、12个基准上达到SOTA性能，推理效率高；模型压缩后性能依然强劲；在模态缺失场景下表现出显著的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: OneTrackerV2证明了单一统一框架可以高效处理多种模态的追踪任务，无需为每种模态单独训练，为多模态追踪提供了一种更高效、可扩展且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal visual object tracking can be divided into to several kinds of tasks (e.g. RGB and RGB+X tracking), based on the input modality. Existing methods often train separate models for each modality or rely on pretrained models to adapt to new modalities, which limits efficiency, scalability, and usability. Thus, we introduce OneTrackerV2, a unified multi-modal tracking framework that enables end-to-end training for any modality. We propose Meta Merger to embed multi-modal information into a unified space, allowing flexible modality fusion and robustness. We further introduce Dual Mixture-of-Experts (DMoE): T-MoE models spatio-temporal relations for tracking, while M-MoE embeds multi-modal knowledge, disentangling cross-modal dependencies and reducing feature conflicts. With a shared architecture, unified parameters, and a single end-to-end training, OneTrackerV2 achieves state-of-the-art performance across five RGB and RGB+X tracking tasks and 12 benchmarks, while maintaining high inference efficiency. Notably, even after model compression, OneTrackerV2 retains strong performance. Moreover, OneTrackerV2 demonstrates remarkable robustness under modality-missing scenarios.

</details>


### [37] [FluxFlow: Conservative Flow-Matching for Astronomical Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03749)
*Shuhong Liu,Xining Ge,Ziteng Cui,Liuzhuozheng Li,Gengjia Chang,Jun Liu,Ziying Gu,Dong Li,Xuangeng Chu,Lin Gu,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FluxFlow框架，结合像素空间流匹配、观测不确定性和源区域重要性权重、无训练维纳正则化测试时校正，以及构建DESI-HST真实基准数据集，实现地面到空间的天文超分辨率，在光度学和科学精度上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面天文观测受限于像素采样分辨率和大气视宁度，导致随机、空间变化的点扩散函数(PSF)，现有方法使用合成训练对无法捕捉真实大气统计特性，且容易产生过平滑重建或幻觉源。

Method: 提出FluxFlow：1) 保守的像素空间流匹配框架，在训练中融入观测不确定性和源区域重要性权重；2) 无训练的维纳正则化测试时校正，抑制幻觉源同时保留恢复细节；3) 构建DESI-HST数据集，包含19,500对真实共配准的地面-空间图像对。

Result: 在光度学和科学精度上均一致优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: FluxFlow通过结合不确定性感知训练和测试时正则化校正，有效解决了地面到空间超分辨率中的幻觉和过平滑问题，真实数据基准验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Ground-to-space astronomical super-resolution requires recovering space-quality images from ground-based observations that are simultaneously limited by pixel sampling resolution and atmospheric seeing, which imposes a stochastic, spatially varying PSF that cannot be resolved through upsampling alone. Existing methods rely on synthetic training pairs that fail to capture real atmospheric statistics and are prone to either over-smoothed reconstructions or hallucination sources with no physical counterpart in the observed sky. We propose FluxFlow, a conservative pixel-space flow-matching framework that incorporates observation uncertainty and source-region importance weights during training, and a training-free Wiener-regularized test-time correction to suppress hallucination sources while preserving recovered detail. We further construct the DESI--HST Dataset, the large-scale real-world benchmark comprising 19,500 real co-registered ground-to-space image pairs with real atmospheric PSF variation. Experiments demonstrate that FluxFlow consistently outperforms existing baseline methods in both photometric and scientific accuracy.

</details>


### [38] [Before Forgetting, Learn to Remember: Revisiting Foundational Learning Failures in LVLM Unlearning Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03759)
*JuneHyoung Kwon,MiHyeon Kim,Eunju Lee,JungMin Yun,Byeonggeuk Lim,YoungBin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）中的隐私遗忘问题，论文指出现有基准存在“欠记忆”和“多跳诅咒”两个根本缺陷，导致遗忘评估不可靠。为此提出ReMem基准：通过原则性数据扩展、推理感知的QA对和多样化视觉上下文确保稳固的基础学习，并引入Exposure指标从模型内部概率分布量化信息擦除深度。实验证明ReMem能可靠诊断LVLMs的学习与遗忘行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLMs遗忘基准使用虚构身份来评估隐私遗忘，但忽略了关键的阶段1失败：模型最初未能有效记忆目标信息，导致后续遗忘评估不可靠。作者诊断出“欠记忆”和“多跳诅咒”是根本原因。

Method: 提出ReMem基准框架：(1) 通过原则性数据扩展确保模型充分记忆目标信息；(2) 设计推理感知的QA对以应对多跳推理；(3) 引入多样化视觉上下文增强泛化能力。同时提出Exposure指标，从模型内部概率分布量化信息擦除深度。

Result: 大量实验表明，ReMem为诊断LVLMs的学习和遗忘行为提供了严格且可信的框架，解决了现有基准中因欠记忆导致的评估不可靠问题。

Conclusion: ReMem通过确保基础学习的可靠性，为LVLMs的隐私遗忘评估建立了更科学、更可信的基准，Exposure指标为量化遗忘深度提供了新的视角。

Abstract: While Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) offer powerful capabilities, they pose privacy risks by unintentionally memorizing sensitive personal information. Current unlearning benchmarks attempt to mitigate this using fictitious identities but overlook a critical stage 1 failure: models fail to effectively memorize target information initially, rendering subsequent unlearning evaluations unreliable. Diagnosing under-memorization and the multi-hop curse as root causes, we introduce ReMem, a Reliable Multi-hop and Multi-image Memorization Benchmark. ReMem ensures robust foundational learning through principled data scaling, reasoning-aware QA pairs, and diverse visual contexts. Additionally, we propose a novel Exposure metric to quantify the depth of information erasure from the model's internal probability distribution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReMem provides a rigorous and trustworthy framework for diagnosing both learning and unlearning behaviors in LVLMs.

</details>


### [39] [GeoTopoDiff: Learning Geometry--Topology Graph Priors through Boundary-Constrained Mixed Diffusion for Sparse-Slice 3D Porous Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03764)
*Yue Shi,Peng Wang,Mingzhe Yu,Yunlong Zhao,Li Liu,Gareth D Hatton,Yan Lyu,Liangxiu Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoTopoDiff，一种基于图扩散的框架，将扩散先验从体素空间转移到混合图状态空间，实现从稀疏CT切片中重建3D多孔微结构，同时建模连续孔隙形态和离散孔喉拓扑，在PTFE和Fontainebleau砂岩上分别减少形态误差19.8%和拓扑输运误差36.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于体素的扩散模型在重建大规模3D多孔微结构时面临挑战，因为需要同时建模连续孔隙形态和离散孔喉拓扑，且依赖完整CT扫描提供拓扑保真的先验，导致吞吐量、拓扑保真度和视野之间存在固有权衡。

Method: 提出GeoTopoDiff框架，将扩散先验从体素空间转移到混合图状态空间（同时包含连续孔隙几何和离散孔喉拓扑），并引入基于稀疏CT切片的拓扑感知部分图先验来约束逆向去噪过程。

Result: 在PTFE和Fontainebleau砂岩上，GeoTopoDiff平均减少形态相关误差19.8%，减少拓扑敏感输运误差36.5%。

Conclusion: 混合图状态空间促进了扩散去噪过程，降低了稀疏观测下的后验不确定性。

Abstract: Diffusion-based voxel prior modelling is challenging for the reconstruction of large-scale 3D porous microstructures. Due to the demanding requirements for simultaneously modelling both the continuous pore morphology and the discrete pore-throat topology, the diffusion models require fully observed CT scans to provide topology-faithful priors, which results in an inherent trade-off among throughput, topological fidelity, and field of view in practical industrial applications. We propose GeoTopoDiff, a graph diffusion-based framework for reconstructing 3D porous microstructures from sparse CT slices. GeoTopoDiff transfers the learning of diffusion priors from a voxel-based space to a mixed graph state space, which simultaneously encompasses continuous pore geometry and discrete pore-throat topology. A topology-aware partial graph prior from sparsely observed CT slices is introduced to constrain the reverse denoising process. Experiments on anisotropic PTFE and Fontainebleau sandstone show that GeoTopoDiff reduces morphology-related errors by 19.8% and topology-sensitive transport errors by 36.5% on average. Our findings suggest that the mixed graph state space promotes the diffusion denoising process to reduce posterior uncertainty under a sparse observations. All models and code have been made publicly available to facilitate the exploration of diffusion models in the field of 3D porous microstructures simulation.

</details>


### [40] [ReLeaf: Benchmarking Leaf Segmentation across Domains and Species](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03784)
*Robert Martinko,Daniel Steininger,Julia Simon,Andreas Trondl,Matthias Blaickner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了基于深度学习的叶片实例分割方法在精准农业中的应用，发现了跨物种和跨场景的泛化能力不足问题，并通过整合多数据集训练和提出包含23种植物的新基准数据集，显著提升了模型泛化性能（mAP50-95达40.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球粮食需求增长和气候压力增大，亟需可持续、精准的农业实践。自动化的个体植物处理依赖于细粒度视觉分析，但叶片级分割在评估作物健康、生长动态、产量潜力和局部胁迫症状方面具有重要价值，却因缺乏专用数据集和系统评估而进展缓慢。

Method: 调研当前数据现状，筛选出四个公开可用的叶片分割数据集；对比单阶段、两阶段和基于Transformer的检测器，确定YOLO26模型配置为实际精准农业任务提供最佳权衡；通过半自动标注从CropAndWeed图像中构建包含23种植物的新基准数据集，并进行广泛的跨域泛化实验。

Result: 在四个现有数据集的测试集上，整合训练模型达到83.9%的mAP50-95；在新基准数据集上达到40.2%的mAP50-95，显著优于单一数据集训练的模型。跨域泛化实验表明，仅靠实验室数据训练的模型在跨物种和跨记录场景下性能大幅下降。

Conclusion: 多样化的叶片分割数据集对于构建鲁棒的精准农业模型至关重要；YOLO26在精度与效率间取得了最佳平衡；当前模型跨域泛化能力仍有待提升。

Abstract: Rising global food demand and growing climate pressure increase the need for sustainable, precise agricultural practices. Automated, individualized plant treatment relies on fine-grained visual analysis, yet leaf-level segmentation remains underexplored despite its value for assessing crop health, growth dynamics, yield potential and localized stress symptoms. Progress is limited by a lack of dedicated datasets, especially regarding species coverage, and by the absence of systematic evaluations of modern instance-segmentation architectures for this task. We address these gaps by surveying current data and identifying four suitable, publicly available leaf-segmentation datasets. Using them, we compare one-stage, two-stage and Transformer-based detectors and identify a YOLO26 model configuration to provide the best trade-off for real-world precision-agriculture tasks. Extensive cross-domain generalization experiments reveal substantial performance drops across plant species and recording setups, especially for models trained solely on laboratory data. To strengthen data availability, we introduce a new benchmark dataset with leaf-level masks for 23 plant species, created via semi-automatic annotation of selected CropAndWeed images. A model trained on all four existing datasets achieves a mean mAP50-95 of 83.9% across their corresponding test sets and 40.2% on our new benchmark, demonstrating improved generalization and highlighting the need for diverse leaf-segmentation datasets in robust precision agriculture.

</details>


### [41] [A Robust Unsupervised Domain Adaptation Framework for Medical Image Classification Using RKHS-MMD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03787)
*Sapna Sachan,Rakesh Kumar Sanodiya,Amulya Kumar Mahto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合迁移学习和RKHS最大均值差异（RKHS-MMD）损失的无监督域适应框架，用于解决医学影像标注瓶颈及跨中心、跨设备的域偏移问题。在来自不同医疗中心的两个胸部X光数据集上，该方法相比未进行域适应训练的模型取得了显著提升，且RKHS-MMD优于标准MMD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像标注需要领域专业知识，且不同医疗中心和成像设备引入的域偏移与模态差异限制了模型的泛化能力，亟需一种无需人工标注的跨域适应方法。

Method: 提出无监督域适应框架，结合迁移学习与基于再生核希尔伯特空间的最大均值差异（RKHS-MMD）损失，联合优化分类损失与RKHS-MMD损失来实现源域和目标域的对齐。

Result: 在两个不同医疗中心的胸部X光数据集上，该方法比未进行域适应训练的模型性能显著提升；对比实验表明RKHS-MMD在缩小模态差距方面优于标准MMD。

Conclusion: RKHS-MMD域适应方法能有效降低医学影像分类中的域偏移问题，减少对人工标注的依赖，在AI驱动的医学诊断中具有广阔应用前景。

Abstract: Labeling medical images is a major bottleneck in the field of medical imaging, as it requires domain-specific expertise, and it gets further complicated due to variability across different medical centers and different imaging devices. Such heterogeneity introduces domain shifts and modality discrepancies, which limits the generalization of trained models. To address this important challenge, we propose an unsupervised domain adaptation framework that combines transfer learning with a Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space based Maximum Mean Discrepancy loss for the alignment of source and target domains. By jointly optimizing classification and RKHS-MMD losses, the methodology enhances generalization to unannotated medical datasets while diminishing reliance on manual annotation. Experimental evaluations presented on two chest X-ray datasets, which are obtained from different medical centers, show outstanding improvements over models trained without adaptation. Furthermore, we perform a comparative study to see that RKHS-MMD performs better than the standard Maximum Mean Discrepancy in reducing modality gap, emphasizing its effectiveness for medical image classification and also its strong capability in advanced AI-driven medical diagnostics.

</details>


### [42] [Enhancing Visual Question Answering with Multimodal LLMs via Chain-of-Question Guided Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03790)
*Quanxing Xu,Ling Zhou,Xian Zhong,Xiaohua Huang,Rubing Huang,Chia-Wen Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoVQD逻辑提示策略和CgRAG框架，通过结合思维链推理与视觉问题分解来增强检索式VQA中MLLM的外部知识获取，在多个基准上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在开放域VQA中依赖外部知识，但检索到的知识准确性和相关性不足，需要更有效的结构化推理与知识获取整合方案。

Method: 提出CoVQD（Chain-of-Thought + Visual Question Decomposition）逻辑提示策略，融合CoT推理与视觉问题分解来引导检索；在此基础上构建CgRAG框架（CoVQD-guided RAG），使MLLM获取更全面一致的外部知识并受益于结构化视觉-文本推理指导。

Abstract: With advances in multimodal research and deep learning, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for a wide range of multimodal tasks. As a core problem in vision-language research, Visual Question Answering (VQA) has increasingly employed MLLMs to improve performance, particularly in open-domain settings where external knowledge is essential. In this work, we aim to further enhance retrieval-based VQA by more effectively integrating MLLMs with structured reasoning and knowledge acquisition. We introduce a logical prompting strategy that fuses Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning with Visual Question Decomposition (VQD), termed CoVQD, to guide retrieval toward more accurate and relevant knowledge for MLLM inference. Building on this idea, we propose a new framework, CoVQD-guided RAG (CgRAG), which enables MLLMs to access more comprehensive and coherent external knowledge while benefiting from structured visual-text reasoning guidance, thereby improving generalization and reliability in complex cross-domain VQA scenarios. Extensive experiments on E-VQA, InfoSeek, and OKVQA benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [43] [Multimodal Learning on Low-Quality Data with Conformal Predictive Self-Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03820)
*Xun Jiang,Yufan Gu,Disen Hu,Yuqing Hou,Yazhou Yao,Fumin Shen,Heng Tao Shen,Xing Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架CPSC，利用共形预测实现多模态学习中低质量数据（模态不平衡与噪声干扰）的自校准，通过表征自校准和梯度自校准两个模块，在六个基准数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习面临低质量数据的挑战，主要表现为模态不平衡和噪声干扰，现有方法常将二者孤立研究，但它们共享一个共同根源：学习过程中对单个模态和实例可靠性的预测不确定性。现有方法缺乏统一框架同时应对这两个问题。

Method: 提出Conformal Predictive Self-Calibration (CPSC)框架，包含：(1) 表征自校准模块：将单模态特征分解为组件，通过共形预测器选择最稳健的组件进行融合以增强特征韧性；(2) 梯度自校准模块：基于实例级可靠性得分在反向传播中重新校准梯度流，引导优化朝更可信方向进行；(3) 共形预测器的自更新策略，确保整个系统在训练过程中协同演化。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，同时在模态不平衡和噪声干扰两种设置下，CPSC框架一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: CPSC通过共形预测驱动的自校准机制，有效统一解决了多模态学习中的模态不平衡和噪声干扰问题，验证了利用预测不确定性进行自我校准的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal learning often grapples with the challenge of low-quality data, which predominantly manifests as two facets: modality imbalance and noisy corruption. While these issues are often studied in isolation, we argue that they share a common root in the predictive uncertainty towards the reliability of individual modalities and instances during learning. In this paper, we propose a unified framework, termed Conformal Predictive Self-Calibration (CPSC), which leverages conformal prediction to equip the model with the ability to perform self-guided calibration on-the-fly. The core of our proposed CPSC lies in a novel self-calibrating training loop that seamlessly integrates two key modules: (1) Representation Self-Calibration, which decomposes unimodal features into components, and selectively fuses the most robust ones identified by a conformal predictor to enhance feature resilience. (2) Gradient Self-Calibration, which recalibrates the gradient flow during backpropagation based on instance-wise reliability scores, steering the optimization towards more trustworthy directions. Furthermore, we also devise a self-update strategy for the conformal predictor to ensure the entire system co-evolves consistently throughout the training process. Extensive experiments on six benchmark datasets under both imbalanced and noisy settings demonstrate that our CPSC framework consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/XunCHN/CPSC.

</details>


### [44] [Identity-Consistent Multi-Pose Generation of Contactless Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03830)
*Zhiyu Pan,Xiongjun Guan,Jianjiang Feng,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IMPOSE框架，通过三阶段流程（潜扩散生成卷指印身份、跨模态翻译转接触式、物理模拟多姿态）合成身份一致的多姿态非接触指纹样本，在UWA和PolyU CL2CB数据库上实现了跨模态匹配SOTA性能（EER分别降至8.74%和2.26%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非接触指纹识别因无物理约束导致自由手指姿态引起严重的非线性几何畸变，造成接触式与非接触式指纹之间的跨模态域差距。现有显式几何校正或图像增强方法在极端姿态下表现脆弱。

Method: IMPOSE三阶段框架：(1) 基于离散码本表示的潜扩散生成卷指印身份；(2) 以Sauvola局部自适应二值化为身份锚点，从卷指印到非接触模态的跨模态翻译；(3) 基于3D手指模型纹理映射和投影的物理多姿态模拟。

Result: 在UWA上EER降至8.74%，PolyU CL2CB上EER降至2.26%。合成数据在DeepPrint和AFRNet等主流表示上均有一致提升，混合真实与合成数据的策略取得最佳结果。

Conclusion: IMPOSE合成的身份一致多姿态样本可有效提升非接触指纹识别的跨模态匹配性能，代码和样本已开源。

Abstract: Contactless fingerprint recognition has gained increasing attention due to its advantages in hygiene and acquisition flexibility. However, the absence of physical contact constraints introduces severe nonlinear geometric distortions caused by free finger poses in 3D space, resulting in a substantial cross-modal domain gap between contactless and conventional contact-based fingerprints. Existing solutions largely rely on explicit geometric correction or image enhancement, which are fragile under extreme pose variations. In this paper, we propose Identity-Consistent Multi-Pose Generation of Contactless Fingerprints (IMPOSE), a physics-inspired framework that synthesizes identity-preserving, multi-pose contactless fingerprint samples to empower recognition models. IMPOSE consists of three stages: (1) rolled fingerprint identity generation via latent diffusion with discrete codebook representations, (2) cross-modal translation from rolled to contactless modality guided by Sauvola-based local adaptive binarization as an identity anchor, and (3) physics-based multi-pose simulation through 3D finger model texture mapping and projection. The generated samples maintain strict identity consistency at the ridge topology level and spatial alignment with standard fingerprint coordinate space. Extensive experiments on the UWA and PolyU CL2CB databases demonstrate that fine-tuning fixed-length dense descriptors (FDD) with IMPOSE-synthesized data achieves state-of-the-art cross-modal matching, reducing EER to 8.74% on UWA and 2.26% on PolyU CL2CB. Synthetic data also yields consistent gains across mainstream representations including DeepPrint and AFRNet, and the hybrid strategy combining synthetic and real data achieves the best overall results. The code and generated samples are available at https://github.com/Yu-Yy/IMPOSE.

</details>


### [45] [Conditions for well-posed color recovery in scattering media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03837)
*Grigory Solomatov,Derya Akkaynak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了散射介质中图像颜色恢复的病态性问题，证明了在理想高光谱相机条件下，利用图像中自然存在的跨像素关系（恢复模式）可以唯一确定颜色解，从而为散射环境下的定量图像分析提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散射介质中的颜色恢复是一个固有的病态逆问题，因为多个解可以解释同一观测结果，且无法在不理解候选解空间的情况下控制预测误差。现有方法缺乏对解空间的系统性理解。

Method: 通过理论分析识别出病态性的两个来源：（1）光谱信号到像素强度的投影；（2）未知的介质参数。证明仅靠传感器改进无法解决介质引起的畸变。提出"恢复模式"（recovery patterns）这一跨像素关系概念，并在理想高光谱相机条件下证明其能限制解为唯一候选。

Result: 证明了在理想高光谱相机条件下，利用图像中自然存在的跨像素恢复模式，可以将解空间限制到唯一候选，从而使颜色恢复问题从病态变为良态。

Conclusion: 该理论为基于第一性原理的新型视觉算法开辟了道路，使得在散射环境中进行定量图像分析成为可能。

Abstract: Recovering scene color from images captured in scattering media is a fundamental inverse problem in optical imaging. Yet the problem is intrinsically ill-posed as multiple solutions can explain the same observation, and prediction error cannot be controlled without understanding the space of candidate solutions. Here, we present sufficient conditions under which color recovery in a scattering medium becomes well-posed. Observing that ill-posedness stems from (i) projection of spectral signals onto pixel intensities, and (ii) unknown medium parameters, we demonstrate that sensor improvements alone cannot resolve medium-induced distortions without additional constraints. We identify recovery patterns, cross-pixel relationships that naturally occur in images, and prove, for an ideal hyperspectral camera, that they restrict the solution to a unique candidate. This opens the door to a new class of vision algorithms grounded in first principles, enabling quantitative analysis of images in scattering environments.

</details>


### [46] [Parameter-Efficient Multi-View Proficiency Estimation: From Discriminative Classification to Generative Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03848)
*Edoardo Bianchi,Antonio Liotta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文讨论了三种近期针对Ego-Exo4D数据集的多视角熟练度估计方法：SkillFormer（参数高效的选择性多视角融合架构）、PATS（保留局部密集运动片段的时序采样方法）和ProfVLM（将熟练度估计转化为条件语言生成，输出熟练度标签和专家式反馈）。这些方法在可训练参数减少高达20倍、训练轮次减少高达3倍的情况下，实现了最先进的准确率，并推动了从封闭集分类向可解释反馈生成的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估一个人执行动作的熟练程度（而非动作类别）对于指导、康复和人才识别至关重要，但熟练度编码在时间、平衡、身体力学和执行方式的细微差异中，且常分布在多视角和短时序事件中，极具挑战性。

Method: SkillFormer：参数高效判别架构，实现选择性多视角融合；PATS：通过保留局部密集的基本运动片段改进时序采样；ProfVLM：将熟练度估计重构为条件语言生成，通过门控跨视角投影器和紧凑语言骨干输出熟练度标签和专家式反馈。

Result: 在Ego-Exo4D上取得最先进准确率，可训练参数减少高达20倍，训练轮次减少高达3倍。

Conclusion: 该领域正朝着高效的多视角系统方向发展，结合了选择性融合、熟练度感知采样和可操作的生成式反馈，从封闭集分类转向可解释反馈生成。

Abstract: Estimating how well a person performs an action, rather than which action is performed, is central to coaching, rehabilitation, and talent identification. This task is challenging because proficiency is encoded in subtle differences in timing, balance, body mechanics, and execution, often distributed across multiple views and short temporal events. We discuss three recent contributions to multi-view proficiency estimation on Ego-Exo4D. SkillFormer introduces a parameter-efficient discriminative architecture for selective multi-view fusion; PATS improves temporal sampling by preserving locally dense excerpts of fundamental movements; and ProfVLM reformulates proficiency estimation as conditional language generation, producing both a proficiency label and expert-style feedback through a gated cross-view projector and a compact language backbone. Together, these methods achieve state-of-the-art accuracy on Ego-Exo4D with up to 20x fewer trainable parameters and up to 3x fewer training epochs than video-transformer baselines, while moving from closed-set classification toward interpretable feedback generation. These results highlight a shift toward efficient, multi-view systems that combine selective fusion, proficiency-aware sampling, and actionable generative feedback.

</details>


### [47] [Stream-R1: Reliability-Perplexity Aware Reward Distillation for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03849)
*Bin Wu,Mengqi Huang,Shaojin Wu,Weinan Jia,Yuxin Wang,Zhendong Mao,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Stream-R1提出了一种可靠性-困惑度感知的奖励蒸馏框架，通过在 rollout 级别和时空元素级别自适应重新加权蒸馏目标，显著提升了流式视频扩散模型的蒸馏质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DMD蒸馏方法统一对待所有rollout、帧和像素的监督信号，忽略了跨rollout的可靠性差异（Inter-Reliability）和跨空间区域及时序帧的困惑度差异（Intra-Perplexity），导致蒸馏质量上限受限。

Method: Stream-R1通过共享的奖励引导机制实现双重自适应加权：Inter-Reliability层面对rollout损失用预训练视频奖励分数的指数进行缩放；Intra-Perplexity层面通过反向传播奖励模型提取逐像素梯度显著性，分解为空间和时间权重，并引入自适应平衡机制防止单一质量维度主导。

Result: 在标准流式视频生成基准上，Stream-R1在视觉质量、运动质量和文本对齐三个维度上均一致优于蒸馏基线，且无需修改架构或增加推理成本。

Conclusion: 通过区分并自适应加权不同粒度的监督信号，Stream-R1有效提升了蒸馏效率和质量，为流式视频扩散模型的加速提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Distillation-based acceleration has become foundational for making autoregressive streaming video diffusion models practical, with distribution matching distillation (DMD) as the de facto choice. Existing methods, however, train the student to match the teacher's output indiscriminately, treating every rollout, frame, and pixel as equally reliable supervision. We argue that this caps distilled quality, since it overlooks two complementary axes of variance in DMD supervision: Inter-Reliability across student rollouts whose supervision varies in reliability, and Intra-Perplexity across spatial regions and temporal frames that contribute unequally to where quality can still be improved. The objective thus conflates two questions under a uniform weight: whether to learn from each rollout, and where to concentrate optimization within it. To address this, we propose Stream-R1, a Reliability-Perplexity Aware Reward Distillation framework that adaptively reweights the distillation objective at both rollout and spatiotemporal-element levels through a single shared reward-guided mechanism. At the Inter-Reliability level, Stream-R1 rescales each rollout's loss by an exponential of a pretrained video reward score, so that rollouts with reliable supervision dominate optimization. At the Intra-Perplexity level, it back-propagates the same reward model to extract per-pixel gradient saliency, which is factored into spatial and temporal weights that concentrate optimization pressure on regions and frames where refinement yields the largest expected gain. An adaptive balancing mechanism prevents any single quality axis from dominating across visual quality, motion quality, and text alignment. Stream-R1 attains consistent improvements on all three dimensions over distillation baselines on standard streaming video generation benchmarks, without architectural modification or additional inference cost.

</details>


### [48] [A Deeper Dive into the Irreversibility of PolyProtect: Making Protected Face Templates Harder to Invert](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03857)
*Vedrana Krivokuća Hahn,Jérémy Maceiras,Sébastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文深入分析了PolyProtect（一种用于保护面部嵌入的生物特征模板保护方法）的“不可逆性”属性，提出了基于余弦距离的数值求解器来更有效地反转PolyProtected模板，并设计了“密钥选择算法”以增强模板的不可逆性。实验表明该算法能显著提高不可逆性，并平衡不同重叠参数下的不可逆性差异，同时发现嵌入元素范围会影响准确性，但可通过归一化预处理加以改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前PolyProtect工作仅基于欧氏距离评估不可逆性，对模板反转的鲁棒性分析不够深入；且随机选择的密钥可能导致某些模板更容易被逆向恢复，存在安全隐患。

Method: (1) 提出基于余弦距离的数值求解器对PolyProtected模板进行逆向攻击；(2) 设计密钥选择算法，通过优化多项式的系数和指数参数来增强模板的不可逆性；(3) 在应用PolyProtect前对嵌入进行归一化预处理以提升准确性。

Result: 基于余弦距离的求解器比欧氏距离更有效地反转PolyProtected模板；密钥选择算法能生成显著更难反转的模板，并使不同重叠参数下的不可逆性趋于一致；归一化嵌入可改善保护域内的准确性。

Conclusion: 该密钥选择算法能有效增强PolyProtect模板的不可逆性，更好地控制不可逆性与准确性之间的权衡，且整个研究可复现。

Abstract: This work presents a deeper analysis of the "irreversibility" property of PolyProtect, a biometric template protection method initially proposed for securing face embeddings. PolyProtect transforms embeddings into protected templates via multivariate polynomials, whose coefficients and exponents are distinct for each subject enrolled in the face recognition system. A polynomial is applied to consecutive sets of elements from a given embedding, where the amount of overlap between the sets is a tunable parameter. We begin our irreversibility analysis by demonstrating that PolyProtected templates are easier to invert using a numerical solver based on cosine distance, as opposed to Euclidean distance (used in the earlier PolyProtect work). To make this inversion more difficult, we then propose a "key selection algorithm", which tries to choose "keys" (coefficients and exponents of the PolyProtect polynomial) that enhance the irreversibility of PolyProtected templates, compared to when the keys are purely random. Our experiments show that this algorithm is effective at generating PolyProtected templates that are significantly more difficult to invert, and that it approximately equalises the irreversibility of PolyProtected templates generated using different "overlap" parameters. This allows for better control of the irreversibility versus accuracy trade-off, known to exist across different overlaps. We also show that accuracy in the PolyProtected domain can be affected by the range in which the embedding elements lie, but that this can be improved by normalizing the embeddings prior to applying PolyProtect. This work is reproducible using our open-source code.

</details>


### [49] [DMGD: Train-Free Dataset Distillation with Semantic-Distribution Matching in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03877)
*Qichao Wang,Yunhong Lu,Hengyuan Cao,Junyi Zhang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需额外微调的双匹配引导扩散框架（DMGD），通过语义匹配（条件似然优化）和分布匹配（最优传输）两种引导机制，在ImageNet子集上超越需要微调的SOTA方法，平均准确率提升2.1%-5.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的数据集蒸馏方法通常需要额外的微调阶段，且有效引导机制研究不足。为此，本文旨在设计一种无需训练、高效引导的扩散框架，以克服上述局限性。

Method: 提出DMGD框架，包含：(1) 语义匹配：通过条件似然优化实现无需辅助分类器的语义对齐；(2) 动态引导机制：在保持语义对齐的同时增强合成数据多样性；(3) 基于最优传输的分布匹配：对齐目标分布结构；(4) 两种高效策略：分布近似匹配与贪心渐进匹配，以最小计算成本实现有效的分布匹配引导。

Result: 在ImageNet-Woof、ImageNet-Nette和ImageNet-1K上，无需微调的方法分别比需要额外微调的SOTA方法平均准确率提升2.1%、5.4%和2.4%。

Conclusion: 所提DMGD框架证明了无需训练引导的扩散式数据集蒸馏方法的有效性，显著超越需要额外微调的现有方法，且计算开销更低。

Abstract: Dataset distillation enables efficient training by distilling the information of large-scale datasets into significantly smaller synthetic datasets. Diffusion based paradigms have emerged in recent years, offering novel perspectives for dataset distillation. However, they typically necessitate additional fine-tuning stages, and effective guidance mechanisms remain underexplored. To address these limitations, we rethink diffusion based dataset distillation and propose a Dual Matching Guided Diffusion (DMGD) framework, centered on efficient training-free guidance. We first establish Semantic Matching via conditional likelihood optimization, eliminating the need for auxiliary classifiers. Furthermore, we propose a dynamic guidance mechanism that enhances the diversity of synthetic data while maintaining semantic alignment. Simultaneously, we introduce an optimal transport (OT) based Distribution Matching approach to further align with the target distribution structure. To ensure efficiency, we develop two enhanced strategies for diffusion based framework: Distribution Approximate Matching and Greedy Progressive Matching. These strategies enable effective distribution matching guidance with minimal computational overhead. Experimental results on ImageNet-Woof, ImageNet-Nette, and ImageNet-1K demonstrate that our training-free approach achieves significant improvements, outperforming state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods requiring additional fine-tuning by average accuracy gains of 2.1%, 5.4%, and 2.4%, respectively.

</details>


### [50] [Raising the Ceiling: Better Empirical Fixation Densities for Saliency Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03885)
*Susmit Agrawal,Jannis Hollman,Matthias Kümmerer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应带宽核密度估计（Abramson方法）结合中心偏差、均匀分量及SOTA显著性模型的混合模型，通过留一受试者交叉验证逐图像优化参数，在多个基准上显著提升注视密度估计的观察者间一致性（中位数对数似然提升5-15%，AUC提升2个百分点），并指出经验注视密度应被视为可改进的估计而非固定真值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前经验注视密度的标准估计方法（固定带宽各向同性高斯KDE）数十年来基本未变，而领域正转向样本级评估（失败案例、逆基准测试、逐图像模型比较），这亟需更可靠的逐图像密度估计方法。

Method: 提出一种混合模型，结合（1）基于Abramson方法的自适应带宽KDE，（2）中心偏差分量，（3）均匀分量，（4）SOTA显著性模型；所有参数通过留一受试者交叉验证逐图像优化。

Result: 在多个基准上获得显著更高的观察者间一致性：中位数逐图像对数似然提升5-15%，AUC提升2个百分点；对失败案例最相关的图像，提升超过25%。

Conclusion: 经验注视密度不应被视为固定真值，而应随方法改进而演进；改进后的估计可揭示SOTA模型仍存在显著的改进空间。

Abstract: Empirical fixation densities, spatial distributions estimated from human eye-tracking data, are foundational to saliency benchmarking. They directly shape benchmark conclusions, leaderboard rankings, failure case analyses, and scientific claims about human visual behavior. Yet the standard estimation method, fixed-bandwidth isotropic Gaussian KDE, has gone essentially unchanged for decades. This matters now more than ever: as the field shifts toward sample-level evaluation (failure case analysis, inverse benchmarking, per-image model comparison), reliable per-image density estimates become critical. We propose a principled mixture model that combines an adaptive-bandwidth KDE based on Abramson's method, center bias and uniform components, and a state-of-the-art saliency model, to capture different spatial and semantic types of interobserver consistency, and optimize all parameters per image via leave-one-subject-out cross-validation. Our method yields substantially higher interobserver consistency estimates across multiple benchmarks, with median per-image gains of 5-15% in log-likelihood and up to 2 percentage points in AUC. For the most affected images -- precisely those most relevant to failure case analysis -- improvements exceed 25%. We leverage these improved estimates to identify and analyze remaining failure cases of state-of-the-art saliency models, demonstrating that significant headroom for model improvement remains. More broadly, our findings highlight that empirical fixation densities should not be treated as fixed ground truths but as evolving estimates that improve with better methodology.

</details>


### [51] [StateVLM: A State-Aware Vision-Language Model for Robotic Affordance Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03927)
*Xiaowen Sun,Matthias Kerzel,Mengdi Li,Xufeng Zhao,Paul Striker,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对视觉-语言模型（VLM）的新型训练策略，通过引入辅助回归损失（ARL）来增强其在机器人任务中的数值推理能力，特别是在目标检测和目标状态定位方面。基于该策略开发的StateVLM模型在多个基准测试中表现优异，同时作者还开源了用于物体状态可供性推理的OSAR基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言模型在机器人应用中受到大语言模型固有局限性的制约，在数值推理方面表现不佳，尤其是在目标检测和目标状态定位等需要精确数值回归的任务中。

Method: 提出一种训练策略，在微调阶段利用边界框解码器输出计算辅助回归损失（ARL），同时保留推理阶段的标准序列预测方式。基于此策略开发了StateVLM模型，并构建了包含1,172个场景、7,746个独立物体的OSAR基准数据集。

Result: 在RefCOCO、RefCOCO+、RefCOCOg基准上，ARL使模型性能平均提升1.6%；在OSAR基准上，StateVLM+ARL相比无ARL版本平均提升5.2%。ARL对于OSAR中的可供性推理任务尤为关键，能增强模型输出的一致性。

Conclusion: 所提出的辅助回归损失训练策略有效提升了VLM在数值推理任务中的表现，StateVLM模型在目标检测、状态定位及可供性推理方面均展现出更优性能。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown remarkable performance in various robotic tasks, as they can perceive visual information and understand natural language instructions. However, when applied to robotics, VLMs remain subject to a fundamental limitation inherent in large language models (LLMs): they struggle with numerical reasoning, particularly in object detection and object-state localization. To explore numerical reasoning as a regression task in VLMs, we propose a novel training strategy to adapt VLMs for object detection and object-state localization. This approach leverages box decoder outputs to compute an Auxiliary Regression Loss (ARL) during fine-tuning, while preserving standard sequence prediction at inference. We leverage this training strategy to develop StateVLM (State-aware Vision-Language Model), a novel model designed to perceive and learn fine-grained object representations, including precise localization of objects and their states, as well as graspable regions. Due to the lack of a benchmark for object-state affordance reasoning, we introduce an open-source benchmark, Object State Affordance Reasoning (OSAR), which contains 1,172 scenes with 7,746 individual objects and corresponding bounding boxes. Comparative experiments on adapted benchmarks (RefCOCO, RefCOCO+, and \mbox{RefCOCOg}) demonstrate that ARL improves model performance by an average of 1.6\% compared to models without ARL. Experiments on the OSAR benchmark further support this finding, showing that StateVLM with ARL achieves an average of 5.2\% higher performance than models without ARL. In particular, ARL is also important for the complex task of affordance reasoning in OSAR, where it enhances the consistency of model outputs.

</details>


### [52] [A Benchmark for Interactive World Models with a Unified Action Generation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03941)
*Jianjie Fang,Yingshan Lei,Qin Wan,Ziyou Wang,Yuchao Huang,Yongyan Xu,Baining Zhao,Weichen Zhang,Chen Gao,Xinlei Chen,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出iWorld-Bench，一个用于评估世界模型物理交互能力的大规模基准测试，包含33万视频片段、2.1k高质量样本、6类任务共4.9k测试样本，评估了14个代表性世界模型，揭示了当前模型在交互能力上的关键局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏大规模数据集和统一基准来评估世界模型在物理交互能力（如距离感知、记忆等）上的表现，阻碍了AGI研究的发展。

Method: 构建包含33万视频片段的大规模数据集，筛选2.1k高质量样本；提出动作生成框架（Action Generation Framework）统一评估，设计6类任务（视觉生成、轨迹跟踪、记忆等），生成4.9k测试样本。

Result: 在6类任务上评估了14个代表性世界模型，识别出关键局限性。

Conclusion: iWorld-Bench为世界模型的交互能力提供了标准化评估，揭示了当前模型的不足，为未来AGI研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Achieving Artificial General Intelligence (AGI) requires agents that learn and interact adaptively, with interactive world models providing scalable environments for perception, reasoning, and action. Yet current research still lacks large-scale datasets and unified benchmarks to evaluate their physical interaction capabilities. To address this, we propose iWorld-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark for training and testing world models on interaction-related abilities such as distance perception and memory. We construct a diverse dataset with 330k video clips and select 2.1k high-quality samples covering varied perspectives, weather, and scenes. As existing world models differ in interaction modalities, we introduce an Action Generation Framework to unify evaluation and design six task types, generating 4.9k test samples. These tasks jointly assess model performance across visual generation, trajectory following, and memory. Evaluating 14 representative world models, we identify key limitations and provide insights for future research. The iWorld-Bench model leaderboard is publicly available at iWorld-Bench.com.

</details>


### [53] [Reservoir property image slices from the Groningen gas field for image translation and segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03942)
*Abdulrahman Al-Fakih,Nabil Sariah,Ardiansyah Koeshidayatullah,SanLinn I. Kaka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于Groningen静态地质模型生成的高分辨率储层属性图像数据集，包含相、孔隙度、渗透率和含水饱和度的二维PNG切片图像，并附带可复现的增强、掩码生成、配对图像构建及基线实验的软件工作流，旨在支持地质图像分析方法的基准测试和储层属性跨域关系研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储层表征工作流日益依赖基于图像和机器学习/深度学习甚至生成式AI方法，但公开可用的、适合可重复基准测试的地质图像数据集仍然有限。

Method: 从Groningen静态地质模型的三维储层网格中生成对齐的二维PNG图像切片，涵盖相、孔隙度、渗透率和含水饱和度四种属性；同时提供可复现的软件工作流用于数据增强、掩码生成、配对图像构建及基线实验。

Result: 产出了对齐的二维储层属性图像语料库及配套的可复现处理工作流，支持下游可视化、分割和图像到图像翻译任务。

Conclusion: 通过将固定图像数据集与可复现处理工作流分离，为地球科学、储层建模和机器学习应用提供了透明的基础设施，有助于推动地质图像分析方法的基准测试。

Abstract: Reservoir characterization workflows increasingly rely on image-based and machine-learning/deep learning or even generative AI approaches, but openly available geological image datasets suitable for reproducible benchmarking remain limited. Here we describe a high-resolution dataset of reservoir-property image slices derived from the Groningen static geological model. The dataset contains aligned two-dimensional PNG images representing facies, porosity, permeability, and water saturation, generated from three-dimensional reservoir grids and prepared for downstream visualization, segmentation, and image-to-image translation tasks. In addition to the deposited original image corpus, we provide an archived software workflow for reproducing augmentation, mask generation, paired-image construction, and example baseline experiments. The resource is designed to support benchmarking of geological image analysis methods and the study of cross-domain relationships among reservoir properties. By separating the fixed image dataset from the reproducible processing workflow, this work provides a transparent foundation for reuse in geoscience, reservoir modeling, and machine-learning applications.

</details>


### [54] [UnAC: Adaptive Visual Prompting with Abstraction and Stepwise Checking for Complex Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03950)
*Yifan Wang,Yun Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UnAC是一种多模态提示方法，通过自适应视觉提示、图像抽象提示和逐步自我检查三个策略，增强LMMs在多步骤推理任务中的表现，在MathVista、MM-Vet和MMMU三个基准上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMMs在视觉感知方面表现强劲，但在需要多步推理的复杂视觉任务中仍然不可靠，缺乏对视觉证据的逐步推理能力。

Method: 提出三种策略：1）自适应视觉提示（adaptive visual prompting），使LMMs聚焦于图像中的显著区域；2）图像抽象提示（image-abstraction prompt），有效提取图像关键信息；3）逐步自我检查（gradual self-checking），通过验证每个分解的子问题及其答案来改进推理过程。

Result: 在MathVista、MM-Vet和MMMU三个公开基准上的大量实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: UnAC作为一种多模态提示方法，能够显著提升GPT-4o、Gemini 1.5和GPT-4V等LMMs在复杂多模态推理任务中的可靠性。

Abstract: Although recent LMMs have become much stronger at visual perception, they remain unreliable on problems that require multi-step reasoning over visual evidence. In this paper, we present UnAC (Understanding, Abstracting, and Checking), a multimodal prompting method that strengthens reasoning for complex multimodal tasks in LMMs (e.g., GPT-4o, Gemini 1.5, and GPT-4V). To improve image understanding and capture fine details, we propose an adaptive visual prompting strategy that enables LMMs to focus on salient regions. We further design an image-abstraction prompt to effectively extract key information from images. In addition, we introduce a gradual self-checking scheme that improves reasoning by verifying each decomposed subquestion and its answer. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks-MathVista, MM-Vet, and MMMU.

</details>


### [55] [Label-Efficient School Detection from Aerial Imagery via Weakly Supervised Pretraining and Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03968)
*Zakarya Elmimouni,Fares Fourati,Mohamed-Slim Alouini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种弱监督框架，利用稀疏位置点和语义分割自动生成标注数据，通过两阶段训练（自标注预训练+少量人工标注微调）实现从航拍图像中检测学校，在低数据条件下仅需50张人工标注图像即可获得优异检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多地区官方记录过时、不完整或不可用，人工标注成本高昂且难以规模化，亟需一种最小化人工标注需求的同时支持全球范围学校检测的方法。

Method: 提出自动标注流水线：利用稀疏位置点和语义分割生成基础设施掩码，再从中生成边界框；采用两阶段训练策略：第一阶段用自动标注数据训练检测器学习学校表征，第二阶段用少量人工标注图像进行微调。

Result: 在低数据条件下表现出色，仅需50张人工标注图像即可取得有竞争力的检测结果，显著降低了对昂贵标注的需求。

Conclusion: 该框架为从卫星图像中高效、可扩展地测绘学校提供了有效方案，支持全球教育和互联互通倡议，所有代码和自动标注数据将公开发布以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Accurate school detection is essential for supporting education initiatives, including infrastructure planning and expanding internet connectivity to underserved areas. However, many regions around the world face challenges due to outdated, incomplete, or unavailable official records. Manual mapping efforts, while valuable, are labor-intensive and lack scalability across large geographic areas. To address this, we propose a weakly supervised framework for school detection from aerial imagery that minimizes the need for human annotations while supporting global mapping efforts. Our method is specifically designed for low-data regimes, where manual annotations are extremely scarce. We introduce an automatic labeling pipeline that leverages sparse location points and semantic segmentation to generate infrastructure masks from which we generate bounding boxes. Using these automatically labeled images, we train our detectors on a first training stage to learn a representation of what schools look like, then using a small set of manually labeled images, we fine-tune the previously trained models on this clean dataset. This two stage training pipeline enables large-scale and strong detection in low-data setting of school infrastructure with minimal supervision. Our results demonstrate strong object detection performance, particularly in the low-data regime, where the models achieve promising results using only 50 manually labeled images, significantly reducing the need for costly annotations. This framework supports education and connectivity initiatives worldwide by providing an efficient and extensible approach to mapping schools from space. All models, training code and auto-labeled data will be publicly released to foster future research and real-world impact.

</details>


### [56] [Enhanced 3D Brain Tumor Segmentation Using Assorted Precision Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04008)
*Adwaitt Pandya,Ozioma C. Oguine,Harita Bhargava,Shrikant Zade*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文使用SegResNet架构和自动混合精度训练进行脑肿瘤三维分割，在肿瘤核心、整体肿瘤和增强肿瘤上分别取得0.84、0.90和0.79的Dice分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑肿瘤严重影响各年龄段人群，早期识别对患者生存至关重要，因此需要高效的自动分割方法。

Method: 采用SegResNet架构进行三维分割，使用自动混合精度训练，以Dice损失函数作为优化目标，Dice系数作为评估指标。

Result: 整体Dice分数为0.84；肿瘤核心Dice分数0.84；整体肿瘤Dice分数0.90；增强肿瘤Dice分数0.79。

Conclusion: SegResNet结合自动混合精度训练在脑肿瘤分割任务上表现良好，尤其对整体肿瘤分割效果最佳。

Abstract: A brain tumor is a medical disorder faced by individuals of all demographics. Medically, it is described as the spread of non-essential cells close to or throughout the brain. Symptoms of this ailment include headaches, seizures, and sensory changes. This research explores two main categories of brain tumors: benign and malignant. Benign spreads steadily, and malignant expresses growth, making it dangerous. Early identification of brain tumors is a crucial factor for the survival of patients. This research provides a state-of-the-art approach to the early identification of tumors within the brain. We implemented the SegResNet architecture, a widely adopted architecture for three-dimensional segmentation, and trained it using the automatic multi-precision method. We incorporated the dice loss function and dice metric for evaluating the model. We got a dice score of 0.84. For the tumor core, we got a dice score of 0.84; for the whole tumor, 0.90; and for the enhanced tumor, we got a score of 0.79.

</details>


### [57] [Large-Scale High-Quality 3D Gaussian Head Reconstruction from Multi-View Captures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04035)
*Evangelos Ntavelis,Sean Wu,Mohamad Shahbazi,Fabio Maninchedda,Dmitry Kostiaev,Artem Sevastopolsky,Vittorio Megaro,Trevor Phillips,Alejandro Blumentals,Shridhar Ravikumar,Mehak Gupta,Reinhard Knothe,Jeronimo Bayer,Matthias Vestner,Simon Schaefer,Thomas Etterlin,Christian Zimmermann,Mathias Deschler,Peter Kaufmann,Stefan Brugger,Sebastian Martin,Brian Amberg,Tom Runia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HeadsUp提出了一种可扩展的前馈方法，能从大规模多相机设置中重建高质量3D高斯人头。该方法采用编码器-解码器架构，将输入视图压缩为紧凑潜在表示，再解码为UV参数化的3D高斯，锚定在标准头部模板上。在包含超10000名受试者的内部数据集上训练，重建质量达到最优，且无需测试时优化即可泛化到新身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理多视角高分辨率输入时，3D高斯数量受限于输入视图的数量和分辨率，难以扩展到大规模多相机设置。同时，缺少大规模多视角人头数据集来支持可泛化的3D人头重建。

Method: 采用编码器-解码器架构，将多视角输入编码为紧凑潜在表示，再解码为UV参数化的3D高斯，锚定在标准头部模板上。UV表示解耦了3D高斯数量与输入图像数量和分辨率，支持使用大量高分辨率输入视图进行训练。

Result: 在超过10000名受试者的内部数据集上训练，达到SOTA重建质量，泛化到新身份无需测试时优化。系统分析了在身份、视图和模型容量上的扩展行为，揭示了质量-计算权衡的实践洞察。

Conclusion: HeadsUp通过UV参数化3D高斯与编码器-解码器架构，有效解决了多视图可扩展性问题，并在潜在空间上演示了生成新3D身份和表情驱动动画两个下游应用。

Abstract: We propose HeadsUp, a scalable feed-forward method for reconstructing high-quality 3D Gaussian heads from large-scale multi-camera setups. Our method employs an efficient encoder-decoder architecture that compresses input views into a compact latent representation. This latent representation is then decoded into a set of UV-parameterized 3D Gaussians anchored to a neutral head template. This UV representation decouples the number of 3D Gaussians from the number and resolution of input images, enabling training with many high-resolution input views. We train and evaluate our model on an internal dataset with more than 10,000 subjects, which is an order of magnitude larger than existing multi-view human head datasets. HeadsUp achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction quality and generalizes to novel identities without test-time optimization. We extensively analyze the scaling behavior of our model across identities, views, and model capacity, revealing practical insights for quality-compute trade-offs. Finally, we highlight the strength of our latent space by showcasing two downstream applications: generating novel 3D identities and animating the 3D heads with expression blendshapes.

</details>


### [58] [Large Language Models are Universal Reasoners for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04040)
*Sucheng Ren,Chen Chen,Zhenbang Wang,Liangchen Song,Xiangxin Zhu,Alan Yuille,Liang-Chieh Chen,Jiasen Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniReasoner框架，利用LLM作为通用推理器，通过先生成粗粒度视觉草图、再进行自我批评式评估、最后将草图和评估信号联合注入扩散模型的方式，弥合文本到图像生成中的“理解-生成鸿沟”，在保持图像质量的前提下提升组合对齐与语义忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一架构（LLM作骨干）在文本到图像生成中存在“理解-生成鸿沟”：模型能准确验证图像是否满足提示，但生成时却无法忠实对齐复杂提示。

Method: UniReasoner三步流程：1) LLM生成离散视觉标记的粗粒度视觉草图；2) LLM自我批评，评估草图与提示的一致性，生成有根据的文本评价；3) 扩散模型以提示、视觉草图和评价为联合条件进行生成，草图减少文本条件的欠指定性，评价将验证转化为可操作的修正信号。

Result: 在相同扩散骨干下，UniReasoner显著提升了组合对齐和语义忠实度，同时保持了图像质量。

Conclusion: 通过利用LLM的推理能力将理解优势转化为生成指导，提供了一种实用且有效的方式来弥合理解-生成鸿沟。

Abstract: Text-to-image generation has advanced rapidly with diffusion models, progressing from CLIP and T5 conditioning to unified systems where a single LLM backbone handles both visual understanding and generation. Despite the architectural unification, these systems frequently fail to faithfully align complex prompts during synthesis, even though they remain highly accurate at verifying whether an image satisfies those same prompts. We formalize this as the \emph{understanding-generation gap} and propose UniReasoner, a framework that leverages the LLM as a universal reasoner to convert its understanding strength into direct generation guidance. Given a prompt, the LLM first produces a coarse visual draft composed of discrete vision tokens. It then performs a self-critique by evaluating the draft for prompt consistency, producing a grounded textual evaluation that pinpoints what needs to be corrected. Finally, a diffusion model is conditioned jointly on the prompt, the visual draft, and the evaluation, ensuring that generation is guided by explicit corrective signals. Each signal addresses a limitation of the other: the draft provides a concrete, scene-level anchor that reduces under-specification in text-only conditioning, while the evaluation turns verification into grounded, actionable constraints that correct omissions, hallucinations, and relational errors. Experiments show that UniReasoner improves compositional alignment and semantic faithfulness under the same diffusion backbone while maintaining image quality, demonstrating a practical way to exploit LLM reasoning to close the understanding-generation gap.

</details>


### [59] [Audio-Visual Intelligence in Large Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04045)
*You Qin,Kai Liu,Shengqiong Wu,Kai Wang,Shijian Deng,Yapeng Tian,Junbin Xiao,Yazhou Xing,Yinghao Ma,Bobo Li,Roger Zimmermann,Lei Cui,Furu Wei,Jiebo Luo,Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次全面综述了基于大基础模型的视听智能（AVI）领域，建立了涵盖理解、生成和交互三类任务的统一分类体系，系统梳理了模态标记化、跨模态融合、自回归/扩散生成、大规模预训练等技术方法，并整理了代表性数据集与评估指标，指出了同步性、空间推理、可控性和安全性等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视听智能（AVI）已成为人工智能的前沿方向，尤其在大型基础模型时代，音频与视觉的联合建模对于理解、可控生成和时序推理至关重要。然而现有文献碎片化严重，任务类型多样、分类体系不统一、评估方法各异，阻碍了系统性比较和知识整合，亟需一篇全面的综述来建立统一框架。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法：首先建立统一的AVI任务分类体系（理解、生成、交互），然后从方法论层面分析模态标记化、跨模态融合、自回归与扩散生成、大规模预训练、指令对齐和偏好优化等技术，最后整理代表性数据集、基准和评估指标，进行结构化比较。

Result: 提供了首个面向大基础模型的AVI全面综述，建立了统一的分类学框架，系统化整理了从理解（语音识别、声音定位）到生成（音频驱动视频合成、视频转音频）再到交互（对话、具身、智能体接口）的任务全景，并识别了同步性、空间推理、可控性和安全性等关键开放挑战。

Conclusion: 本文通过整合碎片化研究领域，为大规模AVI的未来研究提供了基础性参考框架，有助于推动该领域在统一范式下实现系统性进步。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Intelligence (AVI) has emerged as a central frontier in artificial intelligence, bridging auditory and visual modalities to enable machines that can perceive, generate, and interact in the multimodal real world. In the era of large foundation models, joint modeling of audio and vision has become increasingly crucial, i.e., not only for understanding but also for controllable generation and reasoning across dynamic, temporally grounded signals. Recent advances, such as Meta MovieGen and Google Veo-3, highlight the growing industrial and academic focus on unified audio-vision architectures that learn from massive multimodal data. However, despite rapid progress, the literature remains fragmented, spanning diverse tasks, inconsistent taxonomies, and heterogeneous evaluation practices that impede systematic comparison and knowledge integration. This survey provides the first comprehensive review of AVI through the lens of large foundation models. We establish a unified taxonomy covering the broad landscape of AVI tasks, ranging from understanding (e.g., speech recognition, sound localization) to generation (e.g., audio-driven video synthesis, video-to-audio) and interaction (e.g., dialogue, embodied, or agentic interfaces). We synthesize methodological foundations, including modality tokenization, cross-modal fusion, autoregressive and diffusion-based generation, large-scale pretraining, instruction alignment, and preference optimization. Furthermore, we curate representative datasets, benchmarks, and evaluation metrics, offering a structured comparison across task families and identifying open challenges in synchronization, spatial reasoning, controllability, and safety. By consolidating this rapidly expanding field into a coherent framework, this survey aims to serve as a foundational reference for future research on large-scale AVI.

</details>

### [60] [When Should a Language Model Trust Itself? Same-Model Self-Verification as a Conditional Confidence Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02915)
*Aditya Ajay Phalod*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了同模型自验证（self-verification）作为选择性预测置信度信号的有效性，发现其性能高度依赖于任务类型、模型家族、规模和提示形式，不能作为通用不确定性估计器使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究同模型自验证是否能在强似然基线（LL-AVG和LL-SUM）之上提供有实际价值的置信度信号，澄清其在选择性预测中的实用价值。

Method: 在ARC-Challenge和TruthfulQA-MC两个数据集上，针对多个模型家族（Phi-2、Qwen、DeepSeek等）和规模（含7B、8B等），将自验证与LL-AVG、LL-SUM两个基线进行对比。使用AURC和操作点分析评估正确性排序和弃权质量。

Result: 在ARC-Challenge上，自验证对Phi-2和Qwen模型显著优于LL-AVG，Qwen-7B提升最大；在TruthfulQA-MC上，自验证信号不可靠，小模型对提示敏感，DeepSeek-R1-Distill-8B相比LL-AVG性能下降，LL-SUM仍是更强的实用基线。

Conclusion: 自验证不能作为通用不确定性估计器，而应视为条件性置信度信号，其价值取决于任务类型、模型家族、提示形式以及需要超越的基线。

Abstract: Same-model self-verification, prompting a model to audit its own predicted answer, is a plausible confidence signal for selective prediction, but its practical value remains unclear once strong likelihood-based baselines are taken seriously. We evaluate self-verification against two such baselines, LL-AVG and LL-SUM, on ARC-Challenge and TruthfulQA-MC across multiple model families, scales, and prompt variants. We measure not only correctness ranking, but also abstention quality through AURC and operating-point analyses. The results are sharply task- and model-dependent. On ARC-Challenge, self-verification substantially improves over LL-AVG for Phi-2 and the Qwen models, with the largest gains appearing in Qwen-7B. On TruthfulQA-MC, however, the signal is less reliable: smaller models can become prompt-sensitive, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-8B degrades relative to LL-AVG, and LL-SUM often remains the stronger practical baseline. We therefore do not treat self-verification as a general-purpose uncertainty estimator. In this setting, it is better understood as a conditional confidence signal whose value depends on task type, model family, prompt formulation, and, crucially, the baseline it must beat.

</details>


### [61] [Evaluating Reasoning Models for Queries with Presuppositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03050)
*Rose Sathyanathan,Kinshuk Vasisht,Danish Pruthi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型推理模型（LRMs）在面对包含错误预设的用户查询时，能否识别并挑战这些不准确假设。结果显示，相比非推理模型，推理模型准确率略有提升（2-11%），但仍无法挑战26-42%的错误预设，且易受预设表达强度影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量用户查询包含事实不准确的假设，而先前研究表明LLM往往无法质疑这些错误假设，可能强化用户的错误观点。随着模型推理能力的最新进展，研究者希望重新评估推理模型是否能更好地处理这类问题。

Method: 构建了涵盖健康、科学和常识知识领域、具有不同程度预设的查询数据集，对比评估了多个广泛部署的推理模型与非推理模型的性能。

Result: 推理模型准确率比非推理模型高2-11%，但仍无法挑战26-42%的错误预设；推理模型对预设的表达强度仍然敏感。

Conclusion: 尽管推理模型在处理错误预设方面略有改进，但仍有显著局限性，需要进一步研究以增强模型识别和质疑用户错误假设的能力。

Abstract: Millions of users turn to AI models for their information needs. It is conceivable that a large number of user queries contain assumptions that may be factually inaccurate. Prior work notes that large language models (LLMs) often fail to challenge such erroneous assumptions, and can reinforce users' misinformed opinions. However, given the recent advances, especially in model's reasoning capabilities, we revisit whether large reasoning models (LRMs) can reason about the underlying assumptions and respond to user queries appropriately. We construct queries with varying degrees of presuppositions spanning health, science, and general knowledge, and use it to evaluate several widely-deployed models When compared to non-reasoning models, we find that reasoning models achieve a slightly higher accuracy (2-11%), but they still fail to challenge a large fraction (26-42%) of false presuppositions. Further, reasoning models remain susceptible to how strongly the presupposition is expressed.

</details>


### [62] [How Language Models Process Negation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03052)
*Zhejian Zhou,Tianyi Zhou,Robin Jia,Jonathan May*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM处理否定(negation)的机制，发现模型虽具有正确处理的内部组件，但注意力模块的捷径行为导致低准确率；通过消融可大幅提升性能。模型同时采用"抑制性"(抑制被否定概念)和"构建性"(直接构建否定短语表示)两种机制，以后者为主。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM如何从机制层面处理否定这一基础语言现象，理解模型内部组件的行为与准确性之间的关系。

Method: 在Mistral-7B和Llama-3.1-8B上，结合观察性和因果性可解释性技术，分析注意力头行为；通过消融实验验证注意力模块的捷径效应。

Result: 模型包含正确处理否定的组件，但后层注意力模块会促进简单捷径导致错误；消融这些模块可大幅提升否定相关问题的准确率。模型同时使用抑制性和构建性两种否定处理机制，构建性机制更为突出。

Conclusion: LLM内部存在处理否定的竞争性机制共存现象，以构建性计算为主导，深化了对模型内部运算的理解。

Abstract: We study how Large Language Models (LLMs) process negation mechanistically. First, we establish that even though open-weight models often provide wrong answers to questions involving negation, they do possess internal components that process negation correctly. Their poor accuracy is due to late-layer attention behavior that promotes simple shortcuts; ablating those attention modules greatly improves accuracy on negation-related questions. Second, we uncover how models process negation. We consider two hypotheses: models could use attention heads that attend to the phrase being negated and suppress related concepts, or they could directly construct a representation of the entire negative phrase (e.g., representing "not gas" as a vector that promotes liquids and solids). We apply a range of observational and causal interpretability techniques on Mistral-7B and Llama-3.1-8B to show that models implement both mechanisms, with the "constructive" mechanism being more prominent. Combined, our work deepens the understanding of LLMs' internals, highlighting construction-dominant computations and the coexistence of competing mechanisms within LLMs.

</details>


### [63] [The TTS-STT Flywheel: Synthetic Entity-Dense Audio Closes the Indic ASR Gap Where Commercial and Open-Source Systems Fail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03073)
*Venkata Pushpak Teja Menta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过自构建的TTS<->STT飞轮（低成本合成约22,000条实体密集的Indic-English混合语音），在Telugu实体识别任务上将Entity-Hit-Rate从0.027（开源SOTA）和0.16（商业系统）提升至0.473（17倍和3倍提升），同时保持通用朗读性能退化可控（WER仅增加6.6个百分点）。跨语言迁移至Hindi和Tamil也取得显著提升，但均未达到预设目标。消融实验证实几乎所有增益来自合成语料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源和商业ASR系统在特定领域Indic ASR（如数字串、货币金额、地址、品牌名、英印混合码）上表现极差，Telugu实体命中率仅0.027（开源SOTA）和0.16（商业），亟需低成本且有效的解决方案。

Method: 提出自包含TTS<->STT飞轮：1) 使用开源Indic TTS管线以<50美元成本合成约22,000条实体密集的英印混合语音；2) 在vasista22/whisper-telugu-large-v2基础上进行LoRA微调。

Result: Telugu测试集EHR 0.473（开源SOTA的17倍，商业系统的3倍）；FLEURS-Te WER仅增加6.6个百分点；跨语言beta-Hi EHR 0.337（vasista22的7倍），beta-Ta EHR 0.543（vasista22和Deepgram的22倍）；消融实验显示增益100%来自EDSA语料；发现Whisper-large-v3存在Telugu脚本塌陷问题，LoRA可纠正。

Conclusion: 低成本合成数据驱动LoRA微调可大幅提升特定领域Indic ASR性能，但所有模型均未达到预注册的EHR目标（Te 0.75, Hi/Ta 0.65），表明该方向仍需进一步研究。代码、数据全部开源。

Abstract: Niche-domain Indic ASR -- digit strings, currency amounts, addresses, brand names, English/Indic codemix -- is under-served by both open-source SOTA and commercial systems. On a synthesised entity-dense Telugu test set (held-out by synthesis system), vasista22/whisper-telugu-large-v2 (open SOTA) achieves Entity-Hit-Rate (EHR) 0.027 and Deepgram Nova-3 (commercial) 0.16. We close this gap with a self-contained TTS<->STT flywheel: an open-source Indic TTS pipeline synthesises ~22,000 entity-dense Indic-English code-mix utterances at <$50 marginal cost, and a LoRA fine-tune on top of vasista22 achieves EHR 0.473 on the held-out test (17x over open SOTA, 3x over commercial), with read-prose regression bounded to +6.6 pp WER on FLEURS-Te. Cross-language: beta-Hi 0.337 (7x vs vasista22) and beta-Ta 0.543 (22x vs vasista22, 22x vs Deepgram); on Hindi where Deepgram has substantial entity coverage, the flywheel underperforms commercial. All three beta models fall below pre-registered EHR targets (0.75 for Te, 0.65 for Hi/Ta); we report honestly. A native-human-recorded sanity check (n=20 Telugu) confirms transfer to real speech (beta-Te EHR 0.516 on native vs 0.473 on synth). An EDSA-isolation ablation (LoRA on FLEURS-Te alone) yields EHR 0.020 on the same held-out, attributing ~100% of the gain to the EDSA corpus. We additionally report a language-conditional finding: vanilla Whisper-large-v3 has Telugu-specific Script Collapse (SFR 0.46-0.71) that a per-language LoRA corrects (SFR 0.81-0.97), but the recipe is contraindicated on Hindi and Tamil where vanilla SFR >= 0.98. Code, holdouts, predictions, EDSA corpus, and entity dictionaries are released open-source.

</details>


### [64] [MedStruct-S: A Benchmark for Key Discovery, Key-Conditioned QA and Semi-Structured Extraction from OCR Clinical Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03103)
*Yingyun Li,Yu Wang,Haiyang Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了MedStruct-S基准，用于评估OCR临床报告中存在未知键和OCR噪声下的半结构化信息抽取任务，涵盖三种子任务和两类模型范式，实验发现编码器模型在键条件问答上表现最优，同量级下整体优于解码器模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半结构化信息抽取评估忽略了两个关键因素：异构且不完全已知的键表示，以及OCR引入的噪声，导致难以评估模型在真实场景中的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建包含3,582页真实临床报告的MedStruct-S基准，覆盖字段头（键）发现、键条件问答、端到端键值对抽取三个任务，分别评测编码器序列标注+后处理和解码器结构化生成两类范式，共9个模型（0.11B-103B参数）。

Result: 编码器模型在非空键条件问答任务上表现最佳，且同量级下整体优于解码器模型；不控制模型规模时，微调后的解码器模型取得最强整体结果。

Conclusion: MedStruct-S为不同半结构化IE场景下的模型选择和对比提供了可靠且实用的评估基础。

Abstract: Semi-structured information extraction (IE) from OCR-derived clinical reports is crucial for efficiently reconstructing patients' longitudinal medical histories. In practice, this scenario commonly involves three tasks: (i) field-header (key) discovery, (ii) key-conditioned question answering (QA), and (iii) end-to-end key-value pair extraction. However, existing evaluations often under-model two factors: heterogeneous and incompletely known key representations, and OCR-induced noise. This makes it difficult to assess model robustness in real-world settings.
  We present MedStruct-S, a benchmark specifically designed to evaluate these tasks under unknown keys and OCR noise. MedStruct-S contains 3,582 annotated real-world clinical report pages. Using MedStruct-S, we benchmark two representative paradigms: encoder-only sequence labeling with post-processing and decoder-only structured generation, covering four encoder-only and five decoder-only models spanning 0.11B to 103B parameters. Our results show that encoder-only models achieve the best performance for non-null-value key-conditioned QA despite being substantially smaller than decoder-only models. When comparing models of similar order of magnitude, encoder-only models still perform better overall. Without controlling for model scale, fine-tuned decoder-only models deliver the strongest overall results. These findings show that the benchmark provides a reliable and practical basis for selecting and comparing models across different semi-structured IE settings.

</details>


### [65] [Effective Performance Measurement: Challenges and Opportunities in KPI Extraction from Earnings Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03147)
*Rasmus T. Aavang,Rasmus Tjalk-Bøggild,Alexandre Iolov,Giovanni Rizzi,Mike Zhang,Johannes Bjerva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究从非结构化的财报电话会议中提取金融信息，发现基于SEC文件训练的编码器模型难以应对领域迁移，并提出了利用LLM进行开放式信息提取的系统，经人工评估精确率达79.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 财报电话会议是上市公司财务信息的关键来源，但缺乏结构化标签、格式非结构化且使用会话语言，导致信息提取困难，亟需有效的自动化方法。

Method: 评估SEC训练模型在领域迁移下的泛化能力；引入三个新基准（SECB、ECB、ECB-A）；提出利用LLM对非结构化电话会议记录进行开放式提取的系统。

Result: 基于编码器的模型在领域迁移下表现不佳；所提LLM系统经人工评估达到79.7%的精确率。

Conclusion: LLM能够有效从非结构化财报电话会议中提取信息，为该领域的数据分析提供了可行的基线方案。

Abstract: Earnings calls are a key source of financial information about public companies. However, extracting information from these calls is difficult. Unlike the templatic filings required by the U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission (SEC) to report a company's financial situation, earnings conference calls have no built-in labels, are unstructured, and feature conversational language. We explore this challenging domain by assessing the information captured by models trained on SEC filings and in-context learning methods. To establish a baseline, we first evaluate the generalization capabilities of SEC-trained models across established SEC datasets. To support our investigation, we introduce three novel benchmarks: (1) SEC Filings Benchmark (SECB), (2) Earnings Calls Benchmark (ECB), and ECB-A, a subset with 2,460 expert annotation groups to support our qualitative analysis. We find that encoder-based models struggle with the domain shift. Finally, we propose a system utilizing LLMs to perform open-ended extraction from unstructured call transcripts, verified by human evaluation (79.7% precision), providing a baseline for this valuable domain through the consistent tracking of emergent KPIs.

</details>


### [66] [Geometric Deviation as an Unsupervised Pre-Generation Reliability Signal: Probing LLM Representations for Answerability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03196)
*Yucheng Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过分析隐藏状态与可回答参考集的几何偏差，可以在无需标注失败数据或模型输出的情况下，在生成前判断查询是否超出模型知识范围。该方法在数学提示上表现稳定（ROC-AUC 0.78-0.84），但在事实提示上无效，在代码提示上部分泛化，且信号出现在早期层并逐渐衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的語言模型应能在生成前识别超出其知识范围的查询，但现有方法通常需要标注失败数据或依赖模型输出。本文探索是否能利用表示几何特性，在无需这些资源的情况下提供预生成信号。

Method: 测量隐藏状态与可回答参考集的几何偏差，在无需标注失败数据或无模型输出访问的情况下，作为预生成信号。在三个指令微调模型（Llama 3.1-8B, Qwen 2.5-7B, Mistral-7B-Instruct）和三种提示形式（数学、事实、代码）上进行实验。

Result: 几何主要编码任务形式而非可回答性。数学提示中不可回答输入显著偏离可回答质心，ROC-AUC达0.78-0.84，优于简单拒绝基线与自一致性相当。事实提示中无可靠几何信号。代码提示效应量大但方差高。信号出现在早期层并逐渐衰减。

Conclusion: 几何偏差可作为轻量级预生成信号，在具有形式化可回答性约束的结构化领域（如数学）中可靠，但存在明确的泛化边界（事实领域无效）。信号在早期层建立，表明可回答性几何在生成最终阶段之前就已确定。

Abstract: A reliable language model should be able to signal, prior to generation, when a query falls outside its knowledge. We investigate whether representation geometry can provide such a pre-generation signal by measuring the deviation of hidden states from an answerable reference set, requiring no labeled failure data and no access to model outputs.
  Across three instruction-tuned models (Llama 3.1-8B, Qwen 2.5-7B, and Mistral-7B-Instruct) and three prompt forms (Math, Fact, Code), we find that geometry primarily encodes task form. Within mathematical prompts, unanswerable inputs consistently deviate from the answerable centroid, yielding strong separation (ROC-AUC 0.78-0.84). This single-pass pre-generation signal outperforms a simple refusal baseline and compares favorably to self-consistency. It also captures cases where models do not explicitly refuse.
  In contrast, no reliable geometric signal emerges for factual prompts, indicating that the effect is form-conditional rather than universal. Code prompts show large effect sizes with higher variance, suggesting partial generalization beyond mathematical form.
  A layer-wise analysis reveals that the signal arises in early layers and gradually attenuates toward the output. These results suggest that answerability-related geometry is established before the final stages of generation. Together, these findings indicate that geometric deviation can serve as a lightweight pre-generation signal that is reliable in structured domains with formal answerability constraints, with clear boundaries on where it generalizes.

</details>


### [67] [S^2tory: Story Spine Distillation for Movie Script Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03244)
*Mingzhe Lu,Yanbing Liu,Qihao Wang,Jiarui Zhang,Jiayue Wu,Yue Hu,Yunpeng Li,Yangyan Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对电影剧本非线性叙事结构导致的摘要困难，本文提出S^2tory框架，基于叙事学理论通过角色发展轨迹识别核心情节核，利用NEAgent进行理论约束推理，蒸馏知识指导小模型实现高压缩比下的语义保真度摘要生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电影剧本具有非线性、交叉剪辑的叙事结构，传统的表面显著性摘要方法无法有效保留核心故事进展，亟需一种能够理解深层叙事结构的摘要框架。

Method: 提出S^2tory框架，包括：(1) Narrative Expert Agent (NEAgent) 执行理论约束推理，蒸馏其知识以训练小模型识别情节核；(2) 另一个模型利用识别出的情节核生成摘要。该方法基于叙事学理论，通过角色发展轨迹区分驱动叙事的情节核与仅渲染氛围的卫星事件。

Result: 在MovieSum数据集上达到约3.5倍压缩比下的最优语义保真度；在BookSum数据集上的零样本评估证实了强大的跨域泛化能力；人工评估验证了叙事学理论对建模复杂非线性叙事的基础性作用。

Conclusion: 叙事学理论为处理复杂非线性叙事结构提供了不可或缺的理论基础，基于该理论的S^2tory框架在电影剧本摘要任务上显著优于现有方法，且具有良好的跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Movie scripts pose a fundamental challenge for automatic summarization due to their non-linear, cross-cut narrative structure, which makes surface-level saliency methods ineffective at preserving core story progression. To address this, we introduce S^2tory (Story Spine Distillation), a narratology-grounded framework that leverages character development trajectories to identify plot nuclei, the essential events that drive the narrative forward, while filtering out peripheral satellite events that merely enrich atmosphere or emotion. Our Narrative Expert Agent (NEAgent) performs theory-constrained reasoning, whose distilled knowledge conditions a small model to identify plot nuclei. Another model then uses these plot nuclei to generate the summary. Experiments on the MovieSum dataset demonstrate state-of-the-art semantic fidelity at approximately 3.5x compression, and zero-shot evaluation on BookSum confirms strong out-of-domain generalization. Human evaluation further validates that narratological theory provides an indispensable foundation for modeling complex, non-linear narratives.

</details>


### [68] [LLM-XTM: Enhancing Cross-Lingual Topic Models with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03299)
*Minh Chu Xuan,Tien-Phat Nguyen,Linh Ngo Van,Dinh Viet Sang,Nguyen Thi Ngoc Diep,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM-XTM是一个结合LLM引导的主题细化和自一致性不确定性量化的框架，能够在黑盒条件下稳定、可扩展地增强跨语言主题模型，无需依赖双语资源或访问模型内部概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨语言主题模型依赖稀疏的双语资源，主题不连贯且对齐弱；基于LLM的细化方法虽提升可解释性但成本高、易幻觉，且白盒方法需要不可获取的token概率。

Method: 提出LLM-XTM框架，集成LLM引导的主题细化和自一致性不确定性量化，实现黑盒、稳定、可扩展的跨语言主题模型增强。

Result: 在多语言语料库上，LLM-XTM实现了更优的主题连贯性和对齐，同时减少了对双语词典和昂贵LLM调用的依赖。

Conclusion: LLM-XTM通过自一致性不确定性量化，在不依赖白盒信息的条件下有效提升了跨语言主题模型的质量，具备更强的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Cross-lingual topic modeling aims to discover shared semantic structures across languages, yet existing models depend on sparse bilingual resources and often yield incoherent or weakly aligned topics. Recent LLM-based refinements improve interpretability but are costly, document-level, and prone to hallucination, with prior white-box approaches requiring inaccessible token probabilities. We propose LLM-XTM, a framework that integrates LLM-guided topic refinement with self-consistency uncertainty quantification, enabling black-box, stable, and scalable enhancement of cross-lingual topic models. Experiments on multilingual corpora show that LLM-XTM achieves superior topic coherence and alignment while reducing reliance on bilingual dictionaries and expensive LLM calls.

</details>


### [69] [SHIELD: A Diverse Clinical Note Dataset and Distilled Small Language Models for Enterprise-Scale De-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03301)
*Jose D. Posada,David Love,Somalee Datta,Priya Desai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SHIELD数据集（1,394份临床笔记，10,505个PHI标注），通过大语言模型蒸馏为可本地部署的小语言模型，在结构化PHI类别上达到精确率0.88、召回率0.86，解决了云API成本与治理限制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去标识化基准（i2b2 2006/2014）过时且缺乏语义与人口多样性；大语言模型虽零样本表现优异，但云端部署面临计算成本高和PHI数据治理限制。

Method: 采用集合覆盖多样性采样与人工审核构建SHIELD数据集；评估四种LLM（两种专有、两种开源）作为性能上限；通过知识蒸馏将能力迁移至可本地部署的小语言模型。

Result: 蒸馏模型在结构化PHI类别（DATE、DOCTOR、ID、PATIENT、PHONE）上匹配教师模型性能；微平均跨度级精确率0.88、召回率0.86；跨数据集评估显示多样性训练模型在通用结构化PHI上泛化良好。

Conclusion: SHIELD数据集与蒸馏模型可替代云端LLM方案；最佳部署策略为广度覆盖模型与高容量笔记专用模型结合；公开释放数据集与蒸馏后的DeBERTa v3模型。

Abstract: De-identification of clinical text remains essential for secondary use of electronic health records (EHRs), yet public benchmarks such as i2b2 2006/2014 are over a decade old and lack the semantic and demographic diversity of modern narratives. While Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve state-of-the-art zero-shot extraction, enterprise deployment is hindered by compute costs and governance restricting Protected Health Information (PHI) from cloud APIs. We introduce SHIELD (Synthetic Human-annotated Identifier-replaced Entries for Learning and De-identification), a diverse dataset of 1,394 notes with 10,505 gold-standard PHI spans across 9 categories, built via set-cover diversity sampling with human-in-the-loop adjudication. We evaluate four LLMs (two proprietary, two open-weight) to establish a performance ceiling, then distill these capabilities into locally deployable Small Language Models (SLMs). Distributional analysis using Frechet Text Distance and Jensen-Shannon Divergence confirms SHIELD occupies a distinct region of biomedical embedding and vocabulary space versus legacy benchmarks. Our best distilled model matches its teacher on structured PHI categories (DATE, DOCTOR, ID, PATIENT, PHONE) and achieves micro-averaged span-level precision of 0.88 and recall of 0.86 on standard workstation hardware. Cross-dataset evaluation shows diversity-trained models generalize well on universal structured PHI, while institution-specific entities remain hard to transfer, suggesting optimal deployment combines broad-coverage models with specialized models for high-volume notes. We publicly release the SHIELD dataset and the distilled DeBERTa v3 model.

</details>


### [70] [When to Think, When to Speak: Learning Disclosure Policies for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03314)
*Jiaqi Wei,Xuehang Guo,Pengfei Yu,Xiang Zhang,Wanli Ouyang,Siqi Sun,Qingyun Wang,Chenyu You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Side-by-Side (SxS) Interleaved Reasoning通过将披露时机作为可控决策，在标准自回归生成中实现推理与输出的解耦，通过蕴含对齐的轨迹训练（SFT+RL）提升准确率-内容延迟的帕累托权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单流自回归接口中，更新模型状态与不可逆公开承诺耦合，导致"沉默税"：延迟首个任务相关内容或过早流式输出引发偏见。

Method: 提出Side-by-Side (SxS) Interleaved Reasoning，将部分披露与持续私有推理交错进行，仅在内容被推理支持时释放；通过匹配答案前缀与支持推理前缀构建蕴含对齐的轨迹，采用SFT学习双动作语义，RL恢复推理性能。

Result: 在Qwen3-30B-A3B（MoE）和Qwen3-4B（dense）架构上，在AIME25（域内）和GPQA-Diamond（域外）基准测试中，SxS改善了准确率-内容延迟的帕累托权衡。

Conclusion: SxS通过解耦推理与输出时机，有效缓解了"沉默税"问题，在不牺牲推理质量的前提下实现了更灵活的内容披露策略。

Abstract: In single-stream autoregressive interfaces, the same tokens both update the model state and constitute an irreversible public commitment. This coupling creates a \emph{silence tax}: additional deliberation postpones the first \emph{task-relevant} content, while naive early streaming risks premature commitments that bias subsequent generations. We introduce \textbf{\emph{Side-by-Side (SxS)}} Interleaved Reasoning, which makes \emph{disclosure timing} a controllable decision within standard autoregressive generation. SxS interleaves partial disclosures with continued private reasoning in the same context, but releases content only when it is \emph{supported} by the reasoning so far. To learn such pacing without incentivizing filler, we construct entailment-aligned interleaved trajectories by matching answer prefixes to supporting reasoning prefixes, then train with SFT to acquire the dual-action semantics and RL to recover reasoning performance under the new format. Across two Qwen3 architectures/scales (MoE \textbf{Qwen3-30B-A3B}, dense \textbf{Qwen3-4B}) and both in-domain (AIME25) and out-of-domain (GPQA-Diamond) benchmarks, SxS improves accuracy--\emph{content-latency} Pareto trade-offs under token-level proxies (e.g., inter-update waiting).

</details>


### [71] [From prompting to evidence-based translation: A RAG+prompt system for Japanese-Chinese translation and its pedagogical potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03387)
*Wenshi Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种融合语言学分析的RAG+Prompt翻译系统，用于提升日汉名词修饰节构造文的翻译质量。在GPT-4o上，知识库从0扩充到2000条时，平均BLEU从24.28提升至29.96（+23.4%），验证了该方法在可解释性和可审计性方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在日汉翻译中，尤其是包含名词修饰节构造（NMCC）的句子上表现不佳，现有方法缺乏针对该句法结构的专门优化。

Method: 构建RAG+Prompt翻译系统，包含语言学分析模块（输出NMCC内外区分A1和风险预测A2）、基于L2距离的嵌入检索（top-k=5）、增强提示构建，以及GPT-4o生成，无需微调基座模型。

Result: 在66句测试集上，知识库规模从0到2000时，宏观平均句子级BLEU从24.28提升至29.96（+5.68，+23.4%），且性能随知识库增大单调提升。

Conclusion: RAG+Prompt翻译系统以可解释和可审计的方式有效改善了日汉NMCC句的翻译质量，但受限于单一基座模型、单一评测指标和依赖公开语料与商业API。

Abstract: Large language models perform well on high-resource pairs but are less reliable for Japanese-Chinese sentences containing noun-modifying clause constructions (NMCCs). This study evaluates a retrieval-augmented generation RAG+Prompt translation system that integrates linguistic analysis, embedding-based retrieval, prompt construction, and LLM generation without modifying the base model. The analysis module outputs A1 (inner vs. outer NMCC) and A2 (risk predictions: lexical choice/NMCC handling/word order/style/register); top-k = 5 similar Ja-Zh examples (L2 distance) and A1/A2 are inserted into an enhanced prompt. Using GPT-4o and a 66-sentence test set, we compare six knowledge-base sizes (0/100/200/500/1,000/2,000). Macro-averaged sentence-level BLEU (1-4-gram with brevity penalty; cased; Chinese at the character level) is the sole metric. Mean BLEU increases from 24.28 at 0 (RAG disabled) to 29.96 at 2,000 (+5.68; +23.4%). The upward trend holds across sizes, with larger knowledge bases yielding higher scores. We conclude that the RAG+Prompt translation system improves Ja-Zh translation of sentences containing NMCCs in an interpretable and auditable manner. Limitations include one base model, one metric, and reliance on published texts and commercial APIs; future work will broaden genres, language pairs, and evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [72] [Geolocating News about Extreme Climate Events: A Comparative Analysis of Off-the-Shelf Tools for Toponym Identification in German](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03414)
*Brielen Madureira,Mariana Madruga de Brito,Andreas Niekler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 比较三种NER工具（Flair、Spacy、Stanza）在德语新闻文章中识别极端气候事件地理位置的差异，发现工具选择会影响下游任务中关于事件所在国家和媒体关注度的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从文本中确定极端气候事件和灾害的地理位置是气候影响与适应研究中的常见问题，需要评估不同NER工具在此任务上的表现差异。

Method: 对Flair、Spacy和Stanza三种现成NER工具进行对比分析，基于三种方法外在评估它们确定事件发生国家的能力，并追踪差异如何传播到下游任务。

Result: 不同NER工具的输出存在显著差异，这些差异会传播到下游任务，导致关于文档地理焦点的不同判断，进而影响对各国在德国媒体中显著性的结论。

Conclusion: NER工具的选择会直接影响气候事件地理定位的准确性及后续分析结论，研究为德语新闻中极端气候事件的地理位置提取提供了量化对比依据。

Abstract: Determining the geolocation of extreme climate events and disasters in texts is a common problem in climate impact and adaptation research. Named-entity recognition (NER) tools are typically used to identify a pool of toponyms that serve as candidate event locations. In this study, we conduct a comparative analysis of three off-the-shelf NER tools, namely Flair, Spacy and Stanza. We describe and quantify differences between their outputs for German news articles and evaluate them extrinsically based on three methods to determine the country where events took place. We show how their contrasts are propagated into downstream tasks and can yield distinct decisions about a document's geographical focus, which, in turn, can impact conclusions about countries' prominence in German media.

</details>


### [73] [Benchmarking Logistic Regression, SVM, Naive Bayes, and IndoBERT Fine-Tuning for Sentiment Analysis on Indonesian Product Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03439)
*Nabila Zakiyah Zahra,Salwa Farhanatussaidah,Nasywa Nur Afifah,Luluk Muthoharoh,Ardika Satria,Martin C. T. Manullang*

Main category: cs.CL
